Throne of Carina "Lacerta"build 64b08e3edf98rulesVersion 2built-in rules 2.0.0mod format 1carina-modkit 0.2.0

Base text ​

Every key of the game's English text, with its message. A mod gives a key new text in i18n/en/<file>.json; see Limits. An override keeps the base message's arguments, each of the same kind. Proper names (race.*, names.*, tech.field.*.name, tech.app.*.name, ldr.*.name) cannot be overridden and are not listed.

In a message, {name} is an argument. \n is a line break.

help.combat.flier.titleMissiles and Fighters

Click the line to switch the weapon between ready, standby and off; a weapon on standby or off does not fire when you click an enemy.mount (text), status (text or number)

ui.info.ref.entry.hyperPhysics.titleHyper Physics
ui.info.ref.entry.hyperPower.bodyA research line that continues after the Power branch is complete and can be repeated. Each level finished makes the equipment of that branch one step smaller, and adds to your tech value.
ui.info.ref.entry.hyperPower.titleHyper Power
ui.info.ref.entry.hyperSociology.bodyA research line that continues after the Sociology branch is complete and can be repeated. Each level finished makes the equipment of that branch one step smaller, and adds to your tech value.
ui.info.ref.entry.hyperSociology.titleHyper Sociology
ui.info.ref.howToHow to?
ui.info.ref.topic.assignLeaders.nameAssign Leaders
ui.info.ref.topic.assignLeaders.p1Open the Leaders screen. Hire a leader from the pool when one is on offer, then give the leader a post.
ui.info.ref.topic.assignLeaders.p2Colony leaders improve a planet; ship officers improve a ship and its fleet. Select a leader and a colony or ship slot to assign. Leaders cost wages every turn and gain levels with experience.
ui.info.ref.topic.captureShips.nameCapture Ships
ui.info.ref.topic.captureShips.p1A ship that carries marines or troop pods can take an enemy ship by boarding. Bring the target's shields down in battle, then order the boarding party across.
ui.info.ref.topic.captureShips.p2If the marines win, the ship joins your fleet; if they lose, they are lost. Ships with strong crews and armed defenses are harder to take.
ui.info.ref.topic.captureShips.p3Boarding happens only in tactical battles. With Tactical Combat off, battles are resolved automatically and beaten ships are always destroyed, never captured.
ui.info.ref.topic.changeTax.nameChange Tax Rates
ui.info.ref.topic.changeTax.p1Click the treasury box on the map screen to open the tax window and pick a rate. A higher rate turns more of every colony's production into money and leaves less for building.
ui.info.ref.topic.changeTax.p2Taxes do not change morale. The treasury figures follow the new rate at once, and the tax is collected when the turn ends.
ui.info.ref.topic.createColonies.nameCreate New Colonies
ui.info.ref.topic.createColonies.p1Build an Outpost Ship or a Colony Ship on the production list of a colony. Send it to a star that has a free planet and open the colonize window there.
ui.info.ref.topic.createColonies.p2An outpost claims the planet and extends your range and supply. A Colony Ship also lands population units, which start to grow at once.
ui.info.ref.topic.createColonies.p3Monsters guard some systems and block colonization until they are destroyed.
ui.info.ref.topic.curePlague.nameCure a Plague
ui.info.ref.topic.curePlague.p1A plague stops growth on its colony and keeps it under quarantine. It is cured by research.
ui.info.ref.topic.curePlague.p2More research output in the colony shortens it, and the Universal Antidote ends it at once. Keep scientists on the plagued planet if you can.
ui.info.ref.topic.designShips.nameDesign Ships
ui.info.ref.topic.designShips.p1Open the production screen of a colony and press DESIGN. Pick a hull size.
ui.info.ref.topic.designShips.p2A new design gets the best shield, computer, armor and drive you have. Click a system name to swap it, choose NO WEAPON to drop a weapon, and use the add line to mount another where space allows.
ui.info.ref.topic.designShips.p3Press ACCEPT to keep the design, then build it from the same production list. The hull limits space, and every system adds to the cost.
ui.info.ref.topic.destroyComet.nameDestroy a Comet
ui.info.ref.topic.destroyComet.p1A comet announced by the news will strike one of your colonies after a few turns. Park armed ships in orbit of the target planet.
ui.info.ref.topic.destroyComet.p2Every turn the ships wear the comet down by their combined size, and a comet that is destroyed before it arrives does no harm.
ui.info.ref.topic.destroyComet.p3A comet that strikes kills part of the people there and damages buildings.
ui.info.ref.topic.endPirates.nameEnd Pirate Activity
ui.info.ref.topic.endPirates.p1While pirates are active, trade income is cut and freighters are lost along the routes they haunt.
ui.info.ref.topic.endPirates.p2Station warships at your colonies to patrol. The activity ends sooner when the pirates meet armed ships, so keep a fleet near your busiest worlds.
ui.info.ref.topic.getHelp.nameGet More Help
ui.info.ref.topic.getHelp.p1Right click on almost any control to see what it does. Press the right button again, or CLOSE, to dismiss it.
ui.info.ref.topic.getHelp.p2On this console, point at an entry of a category page to read about it. The PLANETS list, the COLONIES list and the Fleets screen answer most questions about what you own.
ui.info.ref.topic.goToStar.nameGo to {star}star (text or number)
ui.info.ref.topic.goToStar.p1{star} is the home of the Vaelori, reached only through a Dimensional Portal.star (text or number)
ui.info.ref.topic.goToStar.p2Find the system of a portal, move a fleet there and use the Attack {star} command in the fleet pop-up. Bring your strongest ships: nothing you meet on the other side is gentle.star (text or number)
ui.info.ref.topic.invadePlanets.nameInvade Planets
ui.info.ref.topic.invadePlanets.p1Load Transports at a colony with Marine Barracks, escort them with warships and fly to the enemy planet.
ui.info.ref.topic.invadePlanets.p2Beat down the defenders and the shields first. When the way is clear, order the landing: the troops fight the garrison, and the winner owns the planet.
ui.info.ref.topic.invadePlanets.p3Better armor, personal shields and rifles raise the strength of your troops. Do not bomb a planet you want to keep: bombing kills its people.
ui.info.ref.topic.moveColonists.nameMove Colonists
ui.info.ref.topic.moveColonists.p1Population can be shipped between your colonies. Use the transport order of a colony, choose how many units go and pick the destination colony.
ui.info.ref.topic.moveColonists.p2Each colonist on the way needs a free freighter, so build Freighters first. Colonists in transit do not work and do not eat, and they arrive after a few turns.
ui.info.ref.topic.moveColonists.p3Use this to fill planets that have room, or to staff an outpost.
ui.info.ref.topic.moveShips.nameMove Ships
ui.info.ref.topic.moveShips.p1Select a fleet on the map or on the Fleets screen and click a star to set its course. The ETA appears at once.
ui.info.ref.topic.moveShips.p2A fleet moves at the speed of its slowest ship. Ships must stay within fuel range of your colonies, so extend your range with outposts and better fuel cells before you go far.
ui.info.ref.topic.moveShips.p3You can split a fleet in the fleet window to send ships to different places.
ui.info.ref.topic.pickColonies.namePick New Colonies
ui.info.ref.topic.pickColonies.p1Each planet has a climate, a size, a gravity and a mineral richness. Check them before you settle.
ui.info.ref.topic.pickColonies.p2Terran, Ocean, Swamp, Arid, Tundra, Desert and Gaia worlds can grow food. A hostile world needs better environment research first.
ui.info.ref.topic.pickColonies.p3Abundant, Rich and Ultra Rich minerals raise what each worker produces. Heavy or light gravity slows growth, production and research unless your race is built for it.
ui.info.ref.topic.pickColonies.p4A planet without farming can still be a fine mine or laboratory if freighters bring it food. The PLANETS list shows the candidates in range.
ui.info.ref.topic.preventSupernovas.namePrevent Supernovas
ui.info.ref.topic.preventSupernovas.p1When a star is about to go nova a countdown starts. Put enough research into that system before it ends and the star calms down.
ui.info.ref.topic.preventSupernovas.p2If the countdown runs out, every planet of the system is scorched and its colonies are lost. Move scientists there, or raise their output with research buildings.
ui.info.ref.topic.refitShips.nameRefit Ships
ui.info.ref.topic.refitShips.p1A refit turns ships of an old design into ships of a newer design with the same hull, so you keep the crews and their experience.
ui.info.ref.topic.refitShips.p2Select the ships on the Fleets screen while they sit in orbit of one of your colonies, press REFIT and choose the new design. The work is paid like any other project of that colony.
ui.info.ref.topicTitleHow to: {name}name (text or number)

ui.modPolicy.draftThis policy covers every mod, race, scenario, art or sound pack and share code made for this game, wherever the project shows, links or lists it.
ui.modPolicy.s1.headMaking mods
ui.modPolicy.s1.p1You may make mods for this game and share them for free. You keep the rights to your work.
ui.modPolicy.s1.p2When the project lists your mod, you allow it to host the mod and to adapt it so it keeps working with new game versions. If mod.json sets allowFeature, you also allow the project to show the mod's name, pictures and short clips in its news, with credit to you.
ui.modPolicy.s10.headCredits and remixes
ui.modPolicy.s10.p1Credit the mods you build on (basedOn in mod.json) and respect their licences. The mod template suggests CC-BY-4.0 for content and MIT for code.
ui.modPolicy.s11.headReporting a mod
ui.modPolicy.s11.p1Use the report link of the mod catalog, or write to the contact address on the catalog page. Say which mod and version, what is wrong and where.
ui.modPolicy.s12.headWhat the project does
ui.modPolicy.s12.p1The project may remove a mod from its lists and tells the author why. Authors who break this policy again and again can be refused. An author can reply to appeal.
ui.modPolicy.s13.headSafety for players
ui.modPolicy.s13.p1The game loads mods as data only and shows each mod's hashes. You can remove a mod at any time; your saves are kept apart from your mods.
ui.modPolicy.s14.headChanges
ui.modPolicy.s14.p1This policy may change. The version in the game and in the mod template is the current one.
ui.modPolicy.s2.headYour own work
ui.modPolicy.s2.p1Share only what you made or may share. List every outside source with its author and licence in the sources field of mod.json.
ui.modPolicy.s3.headNothing from other games
ui.modPolicy.s3.p1Do not include names, pictures, music, sounds, text, logos or other material from other games or media unless you hold the rights to it. That includes material taken from game files by extraction, conversion or AI. Mods that bring back another game's content are not listed or supported.
ui.modPolicy.s4.headNot official
ui.modPolicy.s4.p1Do not present a mod as made, approved or endorsed by the project or by any studio.
ui.modPolicy.s5.headNever allowed
ui.modPolicy.s5.p1Illegal content, sexual content, hate, harassment or threats, content aimed at real people or sharing their private details, and content that promotes terrorism or self-harm.
ui.modPolicy.s6.headFit for the game's players
ui.modPolicy.s6.p1A mod must suit the same players as the game itself. There is no mature-content switch.
ui.modPolicy.s7.headData only
ui.modPolicy.s7.p1A mod holds data, pictures, sound and text. No code, no attempts to break the game's loader, no tracking, and no links that download files from elsewhere.
ui.modPolicy.s8.headGenerative AI
ui.modPolicy.s8.p1Say in mod.json (aiUse) what you made with generative AI. Do not use a real person's likeness, prompts that name a living artist's style, or other games' art as AI input.
ui.modPolicy.s9.headFree mods
ui.modPolicy.s9.p1Mods are free: no paywalls and no paid early access. One link to your own donation page is fine.
ui.modPolicy.section{n}. {head}head (text or number), n (text or number)
ui.modPolicy.titleMod content policy
